HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000296/B003","root_000603/B004","root_000677/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2224346e2af397ad6d23","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:4:1\",\"root_000603\",[\"B004\"]],[\"105:4:2\",\"root_000296\",[\"B003\"]],[\"105:4:4\",\"root_000677\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Tek tek taşlar, yukarıdan boşalan yoğun ve denetimli bir sert yağmur düzeni de kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000043/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_414b557744d0b0ee2ab3","text":"105:5 supplied the effect I did not yet have in 105:4: the stones do not merely hit; they produce عصف B001, broken crop refuse, and ء ك ل B006, eaten-away decay. That later result strengthened the \"hard rain\" reading, because the downpour ends in shredded agricultural debris. It also sharpened the boundary reading: the barrier is successful not only because it blocks, but because the would-be crossing company is converted into residue.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000296/B001","root_000296/B003","root_000296/B004","root_000296/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_441bed77dc0b171c9b29","text":"2. **Stone as boundary and prohibition.** Lexical evidence: ح ج ر B001, keeping back by enclosure/prohibition; ح ج ر B004, enclosed place; ح ج ر B005, lap and sheltered custody; ر ح م B002/B003 in 105:0, kinship/womb; ص ح ب B001 in 105:1, close association. Structural cue: the material \"حجارة\" arrives at the point where the elephant-company is stopped. Assigned role: ح ج ر B003 gives physical stone; ح ج ر B001/B004/B005 activate the stone as barrier, enclosure, and guarded interior. Abductive move: I infer that the projectiles do not only injure bodies but mark a boundary the company cannot cross; the packet supplies the stone/enclosure branches, while I supply their blocking function in the episode. Reading change: \"بحجارة\" reads as interdiction and guarded limit, not only ammunition.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000296/B001","root_000296/B003","root_000296/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_467ecfd9b4256bd5d629","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:4:2\",\"root_000296\",[\"B001\",\"B003\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Taşlar, vurmalarının yanı sıra planın ilerleyişini kapatan somut bir sınır ve yasak işlevi görür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000296/B003","root_000603/B001","root_000677/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4a51ca7449c6741adeec","text":"2. **Discrete projectiles coexist with a storm-like outpouring.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `تَرْمِيهِم` directly realizes `ر م ي` B001 but activates B004, cloud or heavy-rain imagery; fixed `حِجَارَةٍ` carries `ح ج ر` B003, hard stones; fixed `سِجِّيلٍ` activates `س ج ل` B001, “the filled bucket and its outpouring.” **Structural cue:** aerial groups arrive successively in 105:3 and cast plural hard units in 105:4. **Assigned role:** `ح ج ر` B003 preserves the discrete payload, while `ر م ي` B004 and `س ج ل` B001 supply the volumetric pattern of a poured aerial volley. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies no literal cloud or bucket; I infer a material analogy from airborne source, repeated groups, plurality, and outpouring branches. **Reading change:** the ayah supports both targeted bombardment and precipitation-like saturation without collapsing one into the other.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000603/B001","root_000603/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5e59918b541582432d5c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:4:1\",\"root_000603\",[\"B001\",\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Fiziksel atış, planın suçlayıcı yöneliminin onlara maddi bir karşılık olarak dönmesi biçiminde de duyulabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000296/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8127f201280586dd2915","text":"3. **The stones enact interdiction as well as impact.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `حِجَارَةٍ` directly realizes `ح ج ر` B003, hard stone, while dormant B001 is “keeping back by enclosure,” including prevention and prohibition. **Structural cue:** the stones occur after `كَيْدَهُمْ` has been placed in loss and before the group is fixed in a ruined state. **Assigned role:** B003 supplies the physical instrument; B001 supplies its functional role as the barrier that stops further execution of the plan. **Abductive move:** the packet gives impact sequence but does not explicitly say “the stones prevent”; I infer that arrow because the targeted means stands between strategic nullification and terminal transformation. **Reading change:** `حِجَارَةٍ` reads not only as damaging matter but as materialized restraint that closes the plot’s path.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000603/B001","root_000603/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_81f337a55d62cc2aa5e0","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:4:1\",\"root_000603\",[\"B001\",\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Atış, planın yitirdiği hedefe karşı başarıyla sona ulaşan karşı hareket olarak belirginleşir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000677/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_86ca8dc9610474508aa1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:4:4\",\"root_000677\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Taşların niteliği, darbeyi yalnız maddi etki değil, görünür ve güvenceye alınmış bir hüküm gibi de gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000296/B003","root_000603/B001","root_000603/B004","root_000677/B001","root_000677/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8791900f012c710cfdd6","text":"1. **Hard rain: thrown stones as poured-out descent.** Lexical evidence: ر م ي B001, throwing or casting a thing; ر م ي B004, thrown-together cloud or heavy rain cloud; ح ج ر B003, hard stone; س ج ل B001, filled bucket and outpouring; س ج ل B003, unrestricted giving poured out; س م و B004 in 105:0, overhead sky/cover, cloud, rain. Structural cue: birds from above throw stones, while سجل contributes bucket/outpouring imagery and سمو contributes overhead/rain imagery from the opening. Assigned role: رمي supplies projectile motion, حجر supplies hardness, سجل supplies the poured measure, and سمو supplies vertical overhead space. Abductive move: I infer a \"rain of hard matter\" mechanism; the packet supplies the branch ingredients but not the meteorological metaphor as a full causal model. Reading change: the ayah reads as controlled stony downpour, not just individual missiles.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B012","root_000563/B002","root_000677/B004","root_001167/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8b155ded18b3ebb2f75a","text":"3. **Sijjil as record-like verdict.** Lexical evidence: س ج ل B004, record/deed/registration that gathers and secures; ر س ل B002 in 105:3, messenger and message; ر ء ي B012/B013 in 105:1, showing/alerting formula; ف ع ل B001, bringing an act into being. Structural cue: the sent birds carry stones whose source word has a branch of secured writing/registration, and the whole action is displayed to the viewer. Assigned role: سجل B004 gives the stones evidentiary/judicial quality; رسل B002 makes their delivery message-like; رأي makes the result visible. Abductive move: I infer that the material blow also functions as a visible registration of judgment; the packet supplies the lexical record branch, not a fully stated legal scene. Reading change: \"من سجيل\" reads as more than material source: the impact is also a documented/secured act.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000603/B001","root_000603/B006","root_000913/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_94fa98c175ec56f55b12","text":"1. **The cast becomes the successful counterpart to a lost aim.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `تَرْمِيهِم` carries `ر م ي` B001, physical casting at a target, and B006, progressing until a limit or state is reached; earlier `تَضْلِيلٍ` carries `ض ل ل` B001, deviation from aim. **Structural cue:** the object pronoun `هُمْ` gives the casting an explicit target, and the following `فَجَعَلَهُمْ` gives the same pronoun a resulting state. **Assigned role:** `ر م ي` B001 supplies directed launch, B006 supplies endpoint, and `ض ل ل` B001 supplies the failed counter-trajectory. **Abductive move:** contact is not separately stated; I infer successful arrival from target continuity and the immediately resulting transformation. **Reading change:** `تَرْمِيهِم` is sharpened into the precise answering motion to `تَضْلِيل`: what they direct goes astray, while what is directed at them reaches completion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000603/B001","root_000603/B008","root_000913/B001","root_001334/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9e574df08b4026ff1e7c","text":"4. **Thrown accusation returned as material strike.** Lexical evidence: ر م ي B008, throwing accusation in speech; ر م ي B001, physical throwing; ك ي د B002 in 105:2, stratagem; ض ل ل B001, deviation from aim. Structural cue: a plotting group is first placed in misdirection, then becomes the target of throwing. Assigned role: رمي B008 supplies a social/legal \"casting against\"; رمي B001 supplies the physical projectile. Abductive move: I infer a reflexive return: the wrongful directional intent of the plot is answered by a cast-against-them event. This is exploratory because the ayah's surface uses physical throwing, while the speech/accusation branch is activated only by the social logic of كيد and the visible verdict mechanism. Reading change: the throwing can be heard as both impact and charge, without replacing the physical sense.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000603/B006","root_001020/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c56f9d8080a5f6c645b1","text":"105:5’s immediate `فَجَعَلَهُمْ` carries forward the same object pronoun and supplies the unstated endpoint of `ر م ي` B006, retrospectively confirming effective arrival. Its `ع ص ف` B002-B004 branches also made the outpouring analogy in `س ج ل` B001 more visible: the volley behaves like a scattering, sweeping force. This remains a functional analogy, not a claim that 105:4 states literal rain or wind.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
